** STORE-RECORD-VECTOR-RECORD :generic:function:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-SIMD-INTERNALS:STORE-RECORD-VECTOR-RECORD
:ALLOC: HEAP DYNAMIC
:END:
 
#+begin_example

STORE-RECORD-VECTOR-RECORD names a generic function:
  Lambda-list: (OBJECT)
  Derived type: (FUNCTION (T) *)
  Method-combination: STANDARD
  Methods:
    (STORE-RECORD-VECTOR-RECORD (STORE-RECORD))
      Documentation:
        automatically generated reader method

#+end_example

- Called by
  - (FUNCTION-RECORD-REQUIRED-ARGUMENT-RECORDS (STORE-RECORD))

:definitions:
- SYS:CONTRIB;SB-SIMD;CODE;RECORD.LISP.NEWEST
  - SB-SIMD-INTERNALS:STORE-RECORD
:end:
** WITH-PRIMITIVE-ARGUMENT :compiled:macro:function:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-SIMD-INTERNALS:WITH-PRIMITIVE-ARGUMENT
:ALLOC: HEAP DYNAMIC
:END:
 
#+begin_example

WITH-PRIMITIVE-ARGUMENT names a macro:
  Lambda-list: ((SYMBOL VALUE-RECORD) &BODY BODY)
  Source file: SYS:CONTRIB;SB-SIMD;CODE;DEFINE-VOP-FUNCTIONS.LISP.NEWEST

#+end_example

** INSTRUCTION-SET-CASE :compiled:macro:function:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-SIMD-INTERNALS:INSTRUCTION-SET-CASE
:ALLOC: HEAP DYNAMIC
:END:
 
#+begin_example

INSTRUCTION-SET-CASE names a macro:
  Lambda-list: (&BODY CLAUSES)
  Documentation:
    Execute the first clause whose instruction sets are available at run
    time, or signal an error if no clause could be run.
    
    Each clause has to start with an instruction set name, or a list of
    instruction set names, followed by a list of statements.
    
    Example:
    
     (instruction-set-case
       (:sse2 (foo))
       (:avx (bar))
       ((:avx2 :fma) (baz)))
  Source file: SYS:CONTRIB;SB-SIMD;CODE;INSTRUCTION-SET-CASE.LISP.NEWEST

#+end_example

:definitions:
- SYS:CONTRIB;SB-SIMD;CODE;INSTRUCTION-SET-CASE.LISP.NEWEST
:end:
